# Seed 78 on the AMD Ryzen 7 PRO 8840HS - 9 hours from scratch: the weakest of five at the same generation, nothing gained (Iteration 563)

`seed 78 9h then experiment review-checks.py spread then experiment appbench.py --zip ~/Downloads/appbench.zip --sample 16` at 8a065a52f. The chain: done. 32,394 designs, generations 0-126: 19,303 ok, 7,488 stopped after one round, 5,209 screened, 344 died: reach limit at scale 0 - not run, 47 wrong output: apps, 3 died: kernel workload; 3,599 designs an hour.

**The front**: the true one 182 designs - the report's 182, the same.

**The fixed-box hypervolume**: true front 182 (182 inside the box), hypervolume 0.33952 | fastest 0.1493, smallest 14189, least memory 112 KB, fastest at 32 bits 0.1928. **The stopping rule** (stopping.py, results/stopping-series.md): verdict: seed 78 (at its last generation, 126 - the earlier runs there too) gains nothing - the hypervolume 0.33952 against 0.34106 (-0.5%); the fastest 0.1477 against 0.1418 (+4.2%); the smallest 14,189 against 14,051 (+1.0%); the least memory 108 KB against 108 KB (+0 KB); the fastest at 32 bits 0.1818 against 0.1702 (+6.8%) the count: 2 run(s) on the method; the earliest stop after seed 79, if every run until then gains nothing

**The freezes lifted** (lifted.py): prediction 556 (the freezes were right): FAILS - bytehdr, thinhdr, bss, lean at 1 on 95%+: yes; alignhot, msc, sharedcall each on under 10%: no; doesfar and varcall at 1 everywhere: yes

**At 32 bits** (fail32.txt): at 32 bits: 20608 designs measured, 37 failed expected, no fault: 37 of 37 (cpt16's reach at 4-byte cells - Iteration 517); not a known cause: 0


**The deaths**: 47 wrong output: apps, 344 died: reach limit at scale 0 - not run, 3 died: kernel workload. deaths-check.py rebuilt them here (design, generation, in the run, genes, here): 9e2a4b5df1 - 0 - died: kernel workload - - - DIFFERS from s6; d40ff02637 - 1 - died: kernel workload - tracer+nocrossjump - DIFFERS from s6; 1a252d6786 - 1 - died: kernel workload - - - DIFFERS from s6. The control at 29b9999 (before the batch) - deaths-check: 0 of 3 rebuilt compute right here; 3 open.

**The chain on master**: process-checks.sh at 27f3290: build 0, run-tests PASS, validate 7 of 7 IDENTICAL, the gate alive 217 of 217, ans-check 0 of 50 differ (its negative control caught), deaths-check: 0 of 3 rebuilt compute right here; 3 open - OPEN, a correctness issue: seed 78's three cv8 kernel deaths, the JIT off - six lifted values together (HANDOFF.md), the jail PASS.

**The verdict** (rule 1): no comparison step in the chain - the spread at generation 126 (session 65), each run to the same generation: seed 78 the weakest of the five - the fastest 0.1477 against seeds 74-77's 0.1381-0.1418 (4-7% behind), the smallest 14,189 against 14,051-14,173, the spread's hypervolume 0.6339 against 0.6447-0.6619; the memory 108 KB as all. The two runs with the freezes lifted (77, 78) have the two slowest fast ends there (0.1418, 0.1477 against 74-76's 0.1381-0.1389) - two runs: a pattern to watch, not a finding. **The stopping rule** (matched by generation, 562): seed 78 gains nothing - at its last generation, 126, against seed 77 at 126: the hypervolume 0.33952 against 0.34106, the fastest 0.1477 against 0.1418; two runs on the method, the earliest stop after seed 79.

**Prediction 560** (nine hours lose nothing at the fast end and lose at the small end - the tolerance front, hv.py's line): **fails at the fast end, holds at the small end** - the fastest 0.1493 and the hypervolume 0.33952 below seeds 74-77's ranges (0.1363-0.1450; 0.3485-0.3567); the smallest 14,189 above 14,015. Not the hours alone: at generation 126 seed 78 is 4.2% behind seed 77 and behind every run of the spread - the draw. **Prediction 556** (scored at seed 77: holds): lifted.py on seed 78 says its second clause fails - msc on 24% of the true front (enrichment 2.90), sharedcall 78% (0.97), alignhot 16% (1.5-2.2) - while the votes give each a removal vote (8 of 8 over seeds 76-78). lifted.py reads the canonical genome, the votes what is built (express()): a value with no effect drifts on a front. An open question, not a reversal - settled by express() on the lifted genes.

**Corrected at Iteration 564 - not a new fault**: the three are the reach limit at scale 1, known since Iteration 10
(reach_lethal runs cv8 designs with a two-byte DOES> form at scale 1, jailed; 2^15 bytes and the kernel workload's
dictionary is larger), back since doesfar's freeze was lifted (556). gene-narrow.py: doesfar=0 necessary and sufficient
on each; the same form lives at scales 2-3 (474 designs). No correctness issue is open; the paragraph below, as judged
at 563, stands corrected. **The kernel deaths, as judged at 563**: 9e2a4b5df1 (generation 0), d40ff02637 and 1a252d6786 (1), each cv8
with the JIT off, died on the kernel workload - and compute wrong on the code seed 78 ran on (29b9999, the control) and
on master (27f3290). Not the 557 hole (no JIT). All three carry six lifted values at once - bytehdr=0, doesfar=0,
thinhdr=0, bss=0, lean=0, scale=1, each off its frozen value of 556 - and each value alone is common among the living
cv8 interpreter designs (384 to 3,004 of 14,736), the six together on none: a combination the freezes kept out is the
suspect. GOALS.md's stop needs no correctness issue open: until it is fixed the rule cannot stop.

**The remote checks**: the Tegra only (rage, 0.926 over 16); no dream report in this chain.

# Evolved VM designs

27185 designs evaluated, 19303 alive, 5209 screened - not measured (Iteration 200). Speed is the geometric mean of median cpu time / hand-made s6, long workloads (Iteration 100) over kernel, fib, corpus, loop, bubble, ackermann, apps,
relative to s6-cv8b; size is the self-hosting image, binary the stripped engine. sieve, parse is held out.
The front is over three objectives, equal (Iteration 75): speed, binary + image, peak memory - memory within 8 KB a tie (Iteration 77).


The Q-best design (binary + image x run time x memory - the knee; information): 869326522b, speed 0.340, binary + image 22,280, memory 132 KB - Q 0.304 of s6-cv8b's.

## The front's genomes

Each design above, and any other the front was measured again with, as one JSON line: its record without the raw timings. A tool that takes a database (--db) reads this report as one - the lines that are not JSON are skipped - so results/ is all a new chat needs to rebuild a front design.
